function _flog(category, msg, data){ if(!window.FLUXI_DEBUG) return; var ts=new Date().toISOString().slice(11,23); if(data!==undefined){ console.log('[FLUXI:'+category+'] '+ts+' '+msg, data); } else { console.log('[FLUXI:'+category+'] '+ts+' '+msg); } } // Expose inspection helpers window.FLUXI = { // Current state snapshot state: function(){ return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(APP_STATE)); }, // Session info session: function(){ return SESS; }, // All stored data sizes storage: function(){ var out={}; try{ for(var k in localStorage){ if(k.startsWith('fz_')) out[k]=Math.round(localStorage[k].length/1024)+'kb'; } }catch(e){} return out; }, // Active render locks locks: function(){ return Object.keys(_renderLock||{}).filter(function(k){return _renderLock[k];}); }, // Active busy ops busy: function(){ return Object.keys(_busyOps||{}); }, // Active cleanups registered cleanups: function(){ return Object.keys(_cleanups||{}).map(function(k){ return k+'('+_cleanups[k].length+')'; }); }, // Enable/disable debug debug: function(on){ window.FLUXI_DEBUG=on!==false; console.log('FLUXI_DEBUG:',window.FLUXI_DEBUG); }, // Navigate to any view go: function(v){ go(v); }, diagGerente: null, // set after FLUXI init // Current view view: function(){ return currentView; }, // UI temp state â€” backed by persistent _FLUXI_UI var uiState: function(){ return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(_FLUXI_UI)); } }; // Re-attach persistent UI namespace after FLUXI reassignment window.FLUXI.ui=_FLUXI_UI; // â”€â”€ Start SW registration â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ pwaRegister(); // Hide banner if already running as PWA if(pwaIsInstalled()){ var _b=document.getElementById('pwa-banner'); if(_b) _b.style.display='none'; } // ï¿½ï¿½â”€ Guard: protect SPA from @media print side-effects â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ // Some Android browsers apply @media print to the parent when an // iframe or popup prints, triggering .view{display:none!important} // and blanking the app. We capture the active view before printing // and restore it after. (function(){ var _savedView=null; window.addEventListener('beforeprint',function(){ var on=document.querySelector('.view.on'); _savedView=on?on.id:null; }); window.addEventListener('afterprint',function(){ // Re-show the app if it was hidden by print CSS var app=document.getElementById('app'); if(app) app.style.display='flex'; if(_savedView){ var el=document.getElementById(_savedView); if(el) el.style.display='block'; } // Full restore after short delay to var browser finish setTimeout(function(){ if(typeof SESS!=='undefined'&&SESS&&SESS.userId){ var app2=document.getElementById('app'); if(app2){app2.style.display='';app2.className='visible';} if(_savedView){ var el2=document.getElementById(_savedView); if(el2) el2.className='view on'; } } },100); }); })();
